8. components/Waitlist.tsx – Sign‑up Form
tsx
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function Waitlist() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/waitlist', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email }),
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        setSuccess(true);
        setEmail('');
      } else {
        const data = await res.json();
        setError(data.error || 'Something went wrong');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError('Network error. Please try again.');
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <section id="waitlist" className="py-20 bg-blue-50">
      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 text-center">
        <h2 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold mb-4">
          Join the Enterprise Pilot
        </h2>
        <p className="text-xl text-gray-700 mb-8">
          Be among the first 10 enterprises to experience autonomous engineering at scale.
        </p>
        {success ? (
          <div className="bg-green-100 border border-green-400 text-green-700 px-6 py-4 rounded-lg">
            ✅ You're on the list! We'll reach out within 48 hours.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-4 max-w-md mx-auto">
            <input
              type="email"
              placeholder="Enter your work email"
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              required
              className="flex-1 px-4 py-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
            />
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="px-6 py-3 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {loading ? 'Submitting...' : 'Join Pilot'}
            </button>
          </form>
        )}
        {error && <p className="mt-3 text-red-600">{error}</p>}
        <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-gray-500">
          Free pilot • No obligation • Limited slots
        </p>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

11. pages/api/waitlist.ts – Backend Endpoint
ts
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next';

const supabase = createClient(
  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!
);

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  if (req.method !== 'POST') {
    return res.status(405).json({ error: 'Method not allowed' });
  }

  const { email } = req.body;
  if (!email || !email.includes('@')) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Valid email required' });
  }

  const { error } = await supabase
    .from('waitlist')
    .insert([{ email, created_at: new Date().toISOString() }]);

  if (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to sign up' });
  }

  return res.status(200).json({ success: true });
}
12. supabase Table Setup
sql
CREATE TABLE waitlist (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    email TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

Update pages/api/waitlist.ts – Add Email Notification
We'll use Resend (or SendGrid) to send a notification to the team email.

ts
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import { Resend } from 'resend';
import type { NextApiRequest, NextApiResponse } from 'next';

const supabase = createClient(
  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!
);

const resend = new Resend(process.env.RESEND_API_KEY);

export default async function handler(req: NextApiRequest, res: NextApiResponse) {
  if (req.method !== 'POST') {
    return res.status(405).json({ error: 'Method not allowed' });
  }

  const { email } = req.body;
  if (!email || !email.includes('@')) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Valid email required' });
  }

  // 1. Store in Supabase
  const { error } = await supabase
    .from('waitlist')
    .insert([{ email, created_at: new Date().toISOString() }]);

  if (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to sign up' });
  }

  // 2. Send notification to team
  try {
    await resend.emails.send({
      from: 'Pilot Sign-up <notifications@aor.io>',
      to: ['pilot-team@aor.io'],
      subject: 'New Pilot Sign-up',
      html: `<p>A new enterprise has signed up for the pilot:</p>
             <p><strong>Email:</strong> ${email}</p>
             <p>View in Supabase: <a href="${process.env.SUPABASE_DASHBOARD_URL}">dashboard</a></p>`,
    });
  } catch (emailError) {
    console.error('Email notification failed:', emailError);
    // Still return success to user
  }

  // 3. Send confirmation to the user (optional)
  try {
    await resend.emails.send({
      from: 'AOR Pilot Program <pilot@aor.io>',
      to: [email],
      subject: 'Welcome to the AOR Enterprise Pilot',
      html: `<p>Thank you for joining the AOR Enterprise Pilot!</p>
             <p>We'll reach out within 48 hours to schedule your onboarding.</p>
             <p>In the meantime, check out our <a href="https://docs.aor.io">docs</a>.</p>
             <p>– The AOR Team</p>`,
    });
  } catch (emailError) {
    console.error('User confirmation email failed:', emailError);
  }

  return res.status(200).json({ success: true });
}

2. Self‑Serve Demo
We'll add a simple "Try It Now" button on the landing page that opens a WebSocket shell – users can type a requirement and see AOR build it in real time.

Quick Implementation (React component)
tsx
// components/DemoShell.tsx
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function DemoShell() {
  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState('');
  const [logs, setLogs] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [connected, setConnected] = useState(false);
  const [ws, setWs] = useState<WebSocket | null>(null);

  const connect = () => {
    const socket = new WebSocket('wss://demo.aor.io/ws/demo');
    socket.onopen = () => setConnected(true);
    socket.onmessage = (e) => setLogs((prev) => [...prev, e.data]);
    setWs(socket);
  };

  const submit = () => {
    if (!ws || !prompt) return;
    ws.send(JSON.stringify({ prompt }));
    setPrompt('');
  };

  return (
    <div className="border rounded-lg p-4 bg-gray-900 text-white max-w-2xl mx-auto">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
        <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-green-500" />
        <span>{connected ? 'Connected' : 'Disconnected'}</span>
        <button onClick={connect} className="px-3 py-1 bg-blue-600 rounded text-sm">
          {connected ? 'Reconnect' : 'Connect'}
        </button>
      </div>
      <div className="h-64 overflow-y-auto bg-gray-800 p-2 rounded font-mono text-sm mb-3">
        {logs.map((line, i) => (
          <div key={i}>{line}</div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <input
          type="text"
          value={prompt}
          onChange={(e) => setPrompt(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="e.g., Write a Python function to add two numbers"
          className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 bg-gray-700 rounded border border-gray-600 text-white"
          onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && submit()}
        />
        <button onClick={submit} className="px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 rounded hover:bg-blue-700">
          Run
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

⚡ Self‑Serve Demo – "Try It Now" Implementation
We'll add an interactive demo to the landing page where users can submit a task and see AOR work in real‑time – without signing up.

📁 Backend: Demo WebSocket Handler
src/demo/ws.rs – Minimal, secure handler

rust
use axum::{
    extract::{ws::WebSocket, State, WebSocketUpgrade},
    response::IntoResponse,
};
use futures::{SinkExt, StreamExt};
use serde_json::json;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::time::{timeout, Duration};

use crate::orchestrator::Orchestrator;
use crate::task::Task;
use crate::tenant::TenantContext;

pub async fn demo_handler(
    ws: WebSocketUpgrade,
    State(orchestrator): State<Arc<Orchestrator>>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    ws.on_upgrade(|socket| handle_socket(socket, orchestrator))
}

async fn handle_socket(mut socket: WebSocket, orchestrator: Arc<Orchestrator>) {
    // Send welcome message
    let _ = socket.send(axum::extract::ws::Message::Text(
        json!({"type": "info", "content": "🟢 Connected to AOR demo. Enter a requirement."}).to_string()
    )).await;

    // We'll allow only one task per connection
    let mut task_started = false;

    while let Some(Ok(msg)) = socket.recv().await {
        if let axum::extract::ws::Message::Text(text) = msg {
            if let Ok(payload) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&text) {
                if let Some(prompt) = payload.get("prompt").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {
                    if task_started {
                        let _ = socket.send(axum::extract::ws::Message::Text(
                            json!({"type": "error", "content": "Only one task per connection."}).to_string()
                        )).await;
                        continue;
                    }

                    task_started = true;

                    // Create a minimal task
                    let task = Task {
                        id: uuid::Uuid::new_v4(),
                        state: crate::TaskState::Created,
                        requirement: prompt.to_string(),
                        artifacts: vec![],
                        evidence: vec![],
                        created_at: chrono::Utc::now(),
                        metadata: std::collections::HashMap::new(),
                    };

                    let tenant = Arc::new(TenantContext::new(
                        "demo".to_string(),
                        std::path::PathBuf::from("./workspace_demo"),
                    ));
                    // Ensure workspace exists
                    let _ = tenant.ensure_workspace().await;

                    // Spawn a background task to run the orchestrator
                    let orchestrator_clone = orchestrator.clone();
                    let task_id = task.id;
                    let socket_clone = socket.clone();

                    tokio::spawn(async move {
                        let _ = socket_clone.send(axum::extract::ws::Message::Text(
                            json!({"type": "status", "content": "⏳ Processing your request..."}).to_string()
                        )).await;

                        // Run the task with a timeout (60 seconds)
                        let run_result = timeout(
                            Duration::from_secs(60),
                            orchestrator_clone.run_task(task, tenant)
                        ).await;

                        match run_result {
                            Ok(Ok(final_task)) => {
                                let state = format!("{:?}", final_task.state);
                                let artifacts = final_task.artifacts;
                                let evidence = final_task.evidence;

                                let _ = socket_clone.send(axum::extract::ws::Message::Text(
                                    json!({
                                        "type": "complete",
                                        "state": state,
                                        "artifacts": artifacts.iter().map(|a| a.path.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
                                        "evidence": evidence.iter().map(|e| format!("{:?}", e.kind)).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
                                    }).to_string()
                                )).await;
                            }
                            Ok(Err(e)) => {
                                let _ = socket_clone.send(axum::extract::ws::Message::Text(
                                    json!({"type": "error", "content": format!("❌ Task failed: {}", e)}).to_string()
                                )).await;
                            }
                            Err(_) => {
                                let _ = socket_clone.send(axum::extract::ws::Message::Text(
                                    json!({"type": "error", "content": "⏰ Task timed out (60s)."}).to_string()
                                )).await;
                            }
                        }
                    });
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

🌐 Frontend: Demo Component
landing-page/components/DemoShell.tsx – Full interactive component

tsx
import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';

export default function DemoShell() {
  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState('');
  const [logs, setLogs] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [connected, setConnected] = useState(false);
  const [running, setRunning] = useState(false);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);
  const wsRef = useRef<WebSocket | null>(null);
  const logsEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Auto-scroll logs
    if (logsEndRef.current) {
      logsEndRef.current.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
    }
  }, [logs]);

  const connect = () => {
    if (wsRef.current?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) return;
    const socket = new WebSocket('wss://demo.aor.io/ws/demo');
    socket.onopen = () => {
      setConnected(true);
      setLogs([]);
      setDone(false);
      setRunning(false);
      socket.send(JSON.stringify({ prompt: 'ping' })); // just to start
    };
    socket.onmessage = (e) => {
      const data = JSON.parse(e.data);
      if (data.type === 'info') {
        setLogs((prev) => [...prev, `ℹ️ ${data.content}`]);
      } else if (data.type === 'status') {
        setLogs((prev) => [...prev, `⏳ ${data.content}`]);
      } else if (data.type === 'complete') {
        setLogs((prev) => [...prev, `✅ Done! State: ${data.state}`]);
        if (data.artifacts?.length) {
          setLogs((prev) => [...prev, `📄 Artifacts: ${data.artifacts.join(', ')}`]);
        }
        setDone(true);
        setRunning(false);
        socket.close();
      } else if (data.type === 'error') {
        setLogs((prev) => [...prev, `❌ ${data.content}`]);
        setRunning(false);
        setDone(true);
        socket.close();
      }
    };
    socket.onclose = () => {
      setConnected(false);
      if (!done) {
        setLogs((prev) => [...prev, '🔌 Disconnected.']);
      }
    };
    wsRef.current = socket;
  };

  const submit = () => {
    if (!prompt.trim()) return;
    if (!connected) connect();
    // Wait a moment for connection to establish
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (wsRef.current?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
        wsRef.current.send(JSON.stringify({ prompt }));
        setRunning(true);
        setLogs((prev) => [...prev, `📝 Request: "${prompt}"`]);
        setPrompt('');
      }
    }, 300);
  };

  const reset = () => {
    if (wsRef.current) wsRef.current.close();
    setLogs([]);
    setDone(false);
    setRunning(false);
    setConnected(false);
    setPrompt('');
    wsRef.current = null;
  };

  return (
    <div className="border rounded-lg bg-gray-900 text-white p-4 max-w-2xl mx-auto shadow-xl">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className={`h-3 w-3 rounded-full ${connected ? 'bg-green-500' : 'bg-red-500'}`} />
          <span className="text-sm">{connected ? 'Connected' : 'Disconnected'}</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={connect}
            disabled={connected}
            className="px-3 py-1 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 rounded text-sm disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            Connect
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={reset}
            className="px-3 py-1 bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 rounded text-sm"
          >
            Reset
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="h-64 overflow-y-auto bg-gray-800 p-3 rounded font-mono text-sm mb-3">
        {logs.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-gray-500">Connect and submit a requirement to see logs...</div>
        ) : (
          logs.map((line, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="whitespace-pre-wrap border-b border-gray-700 py-1 last:border-0">
              {line}
            </div>
          ))
        )}
        <div ref={logsEndRef} />
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <input
          type="text"
          value={prompt}
          onChange={(e) => setPrompt(e.target.value)}
          onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && !running && submit()}
          placeholder="e.g., Write a Python function to add two numbers"
          className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 bg-gray-700 rounded border border-gray-600 text-white placeholder-gray-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
          disabled={running}
        />
        <button
          onClick={submit}
          disabled={!connected || running || done}
          className="px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 rounded font-medium disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {running ? 'Running...' : 'Run'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

🛡️ Security & Rate Limiting
We'll add a simple rate limiter to prevent abuse:

src/demo/rate_limit.rs (using tokio::sync::Mutex and a HashMap)

rust
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::Mutex;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

pub struct RateLimiter {
    limits: Arc<Mutex<HashMap<String, Vec<Instant>>>>,
    max_requests: usize,
    window: Duration,
}

impl RateLimiter {
    pub fn new(max_requests: usize, window_secs: u64) -> Self {
        Self {
            limits: Arc::new(Mutex::new(HashMap::new())),
            max_requests,
            window: Duration::from_secs(window_secs),
        }
    }

    pub async fn check(&self, ip: &str) -> bool {
        let mut map = self.limits.lock().await;
        let now = Instant::now();
        let entries = map.entry(ip.to_string()).or_insert_with(Vec::new);
        entries.retain(|t| now.duration_since(*t) < self.window);
        if entries.len() >= self.max_requests {
            return false;
        }
        entries.push(now);
        true
    }
}
In the WebSocket handler, we'll check the IP address (from the request) and enforce the limit.

🔐 Security & Encryption
Algorithm: AES‑256‑GCM (authenticated encryption).

Key derivation: PBKDF2 with a random salt (100,000 iterations).

Nonce: Random 12‑byte nonce.

Secret phrase: Aapki taraf se – jo sirf aapko pata ho.

💻 Generate Your Capsule (Offline Python Script)
Aap is script ko apne local machine par run kar sakte hain – internet ki zaroorat nahi.

python
import json
import base64
import hashlib
import secrets
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.ciphers.aead import AESGCM
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.kdf.pbkdf2 import PBKDF2HMAC
from cryptography.hazmat.backends import default_backend

def generate_capsule(secret_phrase: str, conversation_summary: str) -> dict:
    # 1. Derive encryption key
    salt = secrets.token_bytes(16)
    kdf = PBKDF2HMAC(
        algorithm=hashlib.sha256,
        length=32,
        salt=salt,
        iterations=100000,
        backend=default_backend()
    )
    key = kdf.derive(secret_phrase.encode())

    # 2. Encrypt the data
    nonce = secrets.token_bytes(12)
    aesgcm = AESGCM(key)
    plaintext = json.dumps({
        "summary": conversation_summary,
        "insights": [
            "Deterministic core + probabilistic skin",
            "Formal verification (TLA+)",
            "Self‑evolution & meta‑learning",
            "Human‑collaborative HITL",
            "Open‑source ecosystem"
        ],
        "vision": "Autonomous software engineering at scale",
        "created": "2026-08-14T23:59:59Z"
    }).encode()
    ciphertext = aesgcm.encrypt(nonce, plaintext, None)

    # 3. Build capsule
    return {
        "id": "aor-memory-capsule",
        "version": "1.0",
        "timestamp": "2026-08-14T23:59:59Z",
        "salt": base64.b64encode(salt).decode(),
        "nonce": base64.b64encode(nonce).decode(),
        "ciphertext": base64.b64encode(ciphertext).decode(),
        "metadata": {
            "conversation_hash": hashlib.sha256(conversation_summary.encode()).hexdigest(),
            "key_insights": [
                "Deterministic core + probabilistic skin",
                "Formal verification (TLA+)",
                "Self‑evolution & meta‑learning",
                "Human‑collaborative HITL",
                "Open‑source ecosystem"
            ],
            "vision": "Autonomous software engineering at scale"
        }
    }

# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    secret = input("Enter your secret phrase: ")
    summary = """
    (Paste your conversation summary here – I'll generate it for you if needed)
    """
    capsule = generate_capsule(secret, summary)
    with open("memory_capsule.json", "w") as f:
        json.dump(capsule, f, indent=2)
    print("✅ Capsule saved as memory_capsule.json")
    print("⚠️  Store this file securely, and remember your secret phrase!")
